	focus = {
		id = KMT_disband_the_bandit_suppression_headquarters
		icon = GFX_focus_CHI_baojia_system
		prerequisite = { focus = KMT_the_true_enemy focus = KMT_the_foreign_imminent_threat }

		overlay = GFX_focus_fast_overlay_generic_clock
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KMT_taiwan }
			}
			hidden_trigger = {
				IF = {
					limit = { is_ai = yes }
					NOT = { 
						any_neighbor_country = { 
							has_war_with = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = KMT_no_true_enemy_flag
				}
				all_core_state = {
					controller = {
						NOT = {
							OR = {
								original_tag = JAP
								is_ally_with = JAP
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			can_declare_war_on = XIC
			is_neighbor_of = XIC
			IF = {
				limit = {
					XIC = { NOT = { is_subject_of = ROOT }}
				}
				NOT = { is_ally_with = XIC }
			}
		}
		bypass = {
			XIC = {
				OR = {
					exists = no
					has_country_flag = KMT_accepted_relocation
				}
			}
		}
		
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 1
		}
		x = -1
		y = 1
		cost = 8
		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_ANNEXATION }
		relative_position_id = KMT_the_true_enemy
		completion_reward = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = CUF_debuff_tt
			add_to_variable = { global.CHI_CUF = -0.02 }
			add_to_variable = { global.CHI_CUF2 = -0.01 }
			IF = {
				limit = {
					XIC = { is_subject_of = ROOT }
				}
				annex_country = {
					target = XIC
					transfer_troops = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					PRC = { exists = yes }
				}
				XIC = {
					country_event = KMT_event.81
				}
			}
			else = {
				XIC = {
					country_event = KMT_event.812
				}
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = generic_skip_one_line_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = KMT_the_true_enemy_28_days_tt
		}
	}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 2
		}
		search_filters = {FOCUS_FILTER_WAR_SUPPORT FOCUS_FILTER_MANPOWER FOCUS_FILTER_MILITARY_IMPROVEMENT}
		relative_position_id = KMT_finish_the_big_rear_plan
		prerequisite = { focus = KMT_reuse_captured_weaponary2 focus = KMT_finish_the_big_rear_plan }
		completion_reward = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = KMT_nra_dynamic_modifier_tt
			add_to_variable = {
				var = KMT_nra_stab
				value = -0.05
				tooltip = stability_factor_tt
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = KMT_nra_rec_pop
				value = 0.15
				tooltip = conscription_factor_tt
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = r56_newline
			add_war_support = 0.035
			add_manpower = 15000
		}
	}
}
